      FROM (

      FROM (
          SELECT `e`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `e`.`DepartmentId`, `e`.`EmployeeId`, `e`.`RoleId`, `e0`.`EmployeeId` AS `EmployeeId0`, `e0`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`

      ) AS `s`

2026-03-01 13:24:24.362 +05:30 [INF] Relevantz.EEPZ.Data.DBContexts.EEPZDbContext info: 3/1/2026 13:24:24.362 RelationalEventId.CommandExecuted[20101] (Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command) 
      Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[@__p_0='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
      SELECT `s`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `s`.`EmployeeId`, `s`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`, `u0`.`Email`, `r`.`RoleName`, `d`.`DepartmentName`
      FROM (
          SELECT `e`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `e`.`DepartmentId`, `e`.`EmployeeId`, `e`.`RoleId`, `e0`.`EmployeeId` AS `EmployeeId0`, `e0`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          FROM `employeedetailsmaster` AS `e`
          INNER JOIN `employee` AS `e0` ON `e`.`EmployeeId` = `e0`.`EmployeeId`
          LEFT JOIN `userprofile` AS `u` ON `e0`.`EmployeeId` = `u`.`EmployeeId`
          WHERE `e0`.`IsActive`
          ORDER BY `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          LIMIT @__p_0
      ) AS `s`
      LEFT JOIN `userauthentication` AS `u0` ON `s`.`EmployeeId0` = `u0`.`EmployeeId`
      INNER JOIN `role` AS `r` ON `s`.`RoleId` = `r`.`RoleId`
      INNER JOIN `department` AS `d` ON `s`.`DepartmentId` = `d`.`DepartmentId`
      ORDER BY `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`

      Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[@__p_0='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
      SELECT `s`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `s`.`EmployeeId`, `s`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`, `u0`.`Email`, `r`.`RoleName`, `d`.`DepartmentName`
      FROM (
          SELECT `e`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `e`.`DepartmentId`, `e`.`EmployeeId`, `e`.`RoleId`, `e0`.`EmployeeId` AS `EmployeeId0`, `e0`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          FROM `employeedetailsmaster` AS `e`
          INNER JOIN `employee` AS `e0` ON `e`.`EmployeeId` = `e0`.`EmployeeId`
          LEFT JOIN `userprofile` AS `u` ON `e0`.`EmployeeId` = `u`.`EmployeeId`
          WHERE `e0`.`IsActive`
          ORDER BY `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          LIMIT @__p_0
      ) AS `s`
      LEFT JOIN `userauthentication` AS `u0` ON `s`.`EmployeeId0` = `u0`.`EmployeeId`
      INNER JOIN `role` AS `r` ON `s`.`RoleId` = `r`.`RoleId`
      INNER JOIN `department` AS `d` ON `s`.`DepartmentId` = `d`.`DepartmentId`
      ORDER BY `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`

2026-03-01 13:56:05.954 +05:30 [INF] Relevantz.EEPZ.Data.DBContexts.EEPZDbContext info: 3/1/2026 13:56:05.954 RelationalEventId.CommandExecuted[20101] (Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command) 
      Executed DbCommand (1ms) [Parameters=[@__p_0='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
      SELECT `s`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `s`.`EmployeeId`, `s`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`, `u0`.`Email`, `r`.`RoleName`, `d`.`DepartmentName`
      FROM (
          SELECT `e`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `e`.`DepartmentId`, `e`.`EmployeeId`, `e`.`RoleId`, `e0`.`EmployeeId` AS `EmployeeId0`, `e0`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          FROM `employeedetailsmaster` AS `e`
          INNER JOIN `employee` AS `e0` ON `e`.`EmployeeId` = `e0`.`EmployeeId`
          LEFT JOIN `userprofile` AS `u` ON `e0`.`EmployeeId` = `u`.`EmployeeId`
          WHERE `e0`.`IsActive`
          ORDER BY `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          LIMIT @__p_0
      ) AS `s`
      LEFT JOIN `userauthentication` AS `u0` ON `s`.`EmployeeId0` = `u0`.`EmployeeId`
      INNER JOIN `role` AS `r` ON `s`.`RoleId` = `r`.`RoleId`
      INNER JOIN `department` AS `d` ON `s`.`DepartmentId` = `d`.`DepartmentId`
      ORDER BY `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`

      SELECT `s`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `s`.`EmployeeId`, `s`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`, `u0`.`Email`, `r`.`RoleName`, `d`.`DepartmentName`
      FROM (
          SELECT `e`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `e`.`DepartmentId`, `e`.`EmployeeId`, `e`.`RoleId`, `e0`.`EmployeeId` AS `EmployeeId0`, `e0`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          FROM `employeedetailsmaster` AS `e`
          INNER JOIN `employee` AS `e0` ON `e`.`EmployeeId` = `e0`.`EmployeeId`
          LEFT JOIN `userprofile` AS `u` ON `e0`.`EmployeeId` = `u`.`EmployeeId`
          WHERE `e0`.`IsActive`
          ORDER BY `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          LIMIT @__p_0
      ) AS `s`
      LEFT JOIN `userauthentication` AS `u0` ON `s`.`EmployeeId0` = `u0`.`EmployeeId`
      INNER JOIN `role` AS `r` ON `s`.`RoleId` = `r`.`RoleId`
      INNER JOIN `department` AS `d` ON `s`.`DepartmentId` = `d`.`DepartmentId`
      ORDER BY `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`

      SELECT `s`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `s`.`EmployeeId`, `s`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`, `u0`.`Email`, `r`.`RoleName`, `d`.`DepartmentName`
      FROM (
          SELECT `e`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `e`.`DepartmentId`, `e`.`EmployeeId`, `e`.`RoleId`, `e0`.`EmployeeId` AS `EmployeeId0`, `e0`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`

          LEFT JOIN `userprofile` AS `u` ON `e0`.`EmployeeId` = `u`.`EmployeeId`
          WHERE `e0`.`IsActive`
          ORDER BY `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          LIMIT @__p_0
      ) AS `s`

      Executed DbCommand (5ms) [Parameters=[@__p_0='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='Text', CommandTimeout='30']
      SELECT `s`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `s`.`EmployeeId`, `s`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`, `u0`.`Email`, `r`.`RoleName`, `d`.`DepartmentName`
      FROM (
          SELECT `e`.`EmployeeMasterId`, `e`.`DepartmentId`, `e`.`EmployeeId`, `e`.`RoleId`, `e0`.`EmployeeId` AS `EmployeeId0`, `e0`.`EmployeeCompanyId`, `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          FROM `employeedetailsmaster` AS `e`
          INNER JOIN `employee` AS `e0` ON `e`.`EmployeeId` = `e0`.`EmployeeId`
          LEFT JOIN `userprofile` AS `u` ON `e0`.`EmployeeId` = `u`.`EmployeeId`
          WHERE `e0`.`IsActive`
          ORDER BY `u`.`FirstName`, `u`.`LastName`
          LIMIT @__p_0
      ) AS `s`
      LEFT JOIN `userauthentication` AS `u0` ON `s`.`EmployeeId0` = `u0`.`EmployeeId`
      INNER JOIN `role` AS `r` ON `s`.`RoleId` = `r`.`RoleId`
      INNER JOIN `department` AS `d` ON `s`.`DepartmentId` = `d`.`DepartmentId`
      ORDER BY `s`.`FirstName`, `s`.`LastName`
